Don't use for reviewing code diffs (use pr-review), triaging existing review comments (use pr-triage), or copy-editing prose style. It finds the accumulating-memory defect classes (clock drift, cross-chapter state contradictions, prop custody, promise bookkeeping, knowledge-state slips) that no scanner, chapter reviewer, or continuity query can see. - **cold-read**: Write self-contained, timeless answers for a reader with zero context: a multitasking person switching in cold, or someone reading years later. A variant of human-comms. Use when the user says they are multitasking, context-switching, or lost; asks for an atemporal, standalone, or "explain like I know nothing" answer; or wants text that still makes sense in 10 years. Names every subject, states the impact and cause-and-effect chain of every item, uses absolute dates and stable identifiers, and removes references to the conversation. - **cold-read**: Have a fresh agent with none of this conversation read a document before it reaches someone who wasn't there — a spec, implementation plan, PRD, handoff, or design — and report what a newcomer can't follow, which decisions it still leaves to the implementer, and what it proposes building that already exists.
